            covSums[covBase + 3] += weight * (covScratch[3] + dy * dy);
            covSums[covBase + 4] += weight * (covScratch[4] + dy * dz);
            covSums[covBase + 5] += weight * (covScratch[5] + dz * dz);
          }
          rowIndex += 1;
        }
      },
      { chunkBytes: bucketChunkBytes },
    );

    for (let slot = 0; slot < selectedCount; slot++) {
      if (
        !Number.isFinite(weightSums[slot]) ||
        weightSums[slot] <= 1e-12 ||
        counts[slot] === 0
      ) {
        continue;
      }
      const invWeight = 1.0 / weightSums[slot];
      const blockBase = slot * blockWidth;
      const coeffBase = slot * coeffStride + coeffStart;
      for (let c = 0; c < blockWidth; c++) {
        shCoeffs[coeffBase + c] = weightedShBlock[blockBase + c] * invWeight;
      }
    }
    weightedShBlock = null;
  }

  let hasFallbackRows = false;
  for (let slot = 0; slot < selectedCount; slot++) {
    if (
      !Number.isFinite(weightSums[slot]) ||
      weightSums[slot] <= 1e-12 ||
      counts[slot] <= 1
    ) {
      fallbackRowIndex[slot] =
        firstAssigned[slot] >= 0 ? firstAssigned[slot] : selectedRows[slot];
      hasFallbackRows = true;
      continue;
    }

    const invWeight = 1.0 / weightSums[slot];
    const covBase = slot * 6;
    if (!scaleLog) {
      scaleLog = new Float32Array(selectedCount * 3);
      quats = new Float32Array(selectedCount * 4);
    }
    covarianceToScaleQuat(
      Math.max(covSums[covBase + 0] * invWeight, 1e-20),
      covSums[covBase + 1] * invWeight,
      covSums[covBase + 2] * invWeight,
      Math.max(covSums[covBase + 3] * invWeight, 1e-20),
      covSums[covBase + 4] * invWeight,
      Math.max(covSums[covBase + 5] * invWeight, 1e-20),
      scaleLog,
      slot * 3,
      quats,
      slot * 4,
    );
    const scaleBase = slot * 3;
    const sx = Math.exp(scaleLog[scaleBase + 0]);
    const sy = Math.exp(scaleLog[scaleBase + 1]);
    const sz = Math.exp(scaleLog[scaleBase + 2]);
    opacity[slot] = clampMergedOpacity(
      weightSums[slot] / Math.max(ellipsoidAreaFromScales(sx, sy, sz), 1e-30),
    );
    outputRadius[slot] = Math.fround(
      computeThreeSigmaAabbBlendedRadiusAt(scaleLog, slot * 3, quats, slot * 4),
    );
  }

  if (!scaleLog) {
    scaleLog = new Float32Array(selectedCount * 3);
    quats = new Float32Array(selectedCount * 4);
  }
  covSums = null;

  if (hasFallbackRows) {
    await materializeBucketRowsToSlots(
      entries,
      coeffCount,
      fallbackRowIndex,
      positions,
      scaleLog,
      quats,
      opacity,
      shCoeffs,
      { bucketChunkBytes },
    );
    for (let slot = 0; slot < selectedCount; slot++) {
      if (fallbackRowIndex[slot] < 0) {
        continue;
      }
      outputRadius[slot] = Math.fround(
        computeThreeSigmaAabbBlendedRadiusAt(
          scaleLog,
          slot * 3,
          quats,
          slot * 4,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  const cloud = new GaussianCloud(
    positions,
    scaleLog,
    quats,
    opacity,
    shCoeffs,
    null,
  );
  cloud._shDegree = shDegreeFromCoeffCount(coeffCount);
  return { cloud, outputRadius };
}

module.exports = {
  loadBucketSimplifyCoreFromEntries,
  mergeSelectedBucketRowsToCloud,
  rowRadiusOrScratch,
};
```

### src/tiling/content-targets.js
```
const {
  normalizeSplatTargetCount,
  constrainTargetSplatCount,
} = require('./simplify-plan');
const { samplingDivisorForDepth } = require('./geometric-error');
const {
  nodeBuildState,
  existingNodeBuildState,
} = require('./adaptive');

function targetSplatCountForDepth(
  depth,
  lodMaxDepth,
  samplingRatePerLevel,
  splatCount,
) {
  const divisor = samplingDivisorForDepth(
    depth,
    lodMaxDepth,
    samplingRatePerLevel,
  );
  return Math.max(1, Math.min(splatCount, Math.ceil(splatCount / divisor)));
}

function resolveNodeContentTargetForParams(
  node,
  lodMaxDepth,
  samplingRatePerLevel,
  inputSplatCount,
  occupiedChildCount = node.occupiedChildCount,
) {
  if (
    Number.isFinite(node.contentTargetOverride) &&
    node.contentTargetOverride > 0
  ) {
    return normalizeSplatTargetCount(
      node.contentTargetOverride,
      inputSplatCount,
    );
  }
  const targetRaw = targetSplatCountForDepth(
    node.depth,
    lodMaxDepth,
    samplingRatePerLevel,
    node.count,
  );
  return constrainTargetSplatCount(
    targetRaw,
    inputSplatCount,
    occupiedChildCount,
  );
}

function resolveNodeContentTarget(node, ctx, inputSplatCount) {
  return resolveNodeContentTargetForParams(
    node,
    ctx.lodMaxDepth,
    ctx.params.samplingRatePerLevel,
    inputSplatCount,
  );
}

function resolveVirtualNodeContentTarget(
  node,
  lodMaxDepth,
  samplingRatePerLevel,
) {
  const targetRaw =
    Number.isFinite(node.contentTargetOverride) &&
    node.contentTargetOverride > 0
      ? node.contentTargetOverride
      : targetSplatCountForDepth(
          node.depth,
          lodMaxDepth,
          samplingRatePerLevel,
          node.count,
        );
  return constrainTargetSplatCount(targetRaw, node.count, node.children.length);
}

function minimumContentTargetForVirtualBudget(node) {
  const build = existingNodeBuildState(node);
  if (
    build &&
    Number.isInteger(build.virtualBudgetMinimum) &&
    build.virtualBudgetMinimum >= 0
  ) {
    return build.virtualBudgetMinimum;
  }
  return computeVirtualBudgetMinimums(node);
}

function computeVirtualBudgetMinimums(node) {
  let minimum;
  if (!node.virtual) {
    minimum = node.count > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  } else {
    minimum = node.children.reduce(
      (sum, child) => sum + computeVirtualBudgetMinimums(child),
      0,
    );
  }
  nodeBuildState(node).virtualBudgetMinimum = minimum;
  return minimum;
}

function childSlotSortKey(child) {
  return Number.isInteger(child.childSlot) ? child.childSlot : 0;
}

function compareContentAllocationPriority(a, b) {
  if (b.remainder !== a.remainder) return b.remainder - a.remainder;
  if (b.child.count !== a.child.count) return b.child.count - a.child.count;
  return childSlotSortKey(a.child) - childSlotSortKey(b.child);
}

function distributeRemainingByPriority(allocations, remaining) {
  const active = allocations
    .filter((entry) => entry.capacity > 0)
    .sort(compareContentAllocationPriority);
  let activeCount = active.length;
  while (remaining > 0 && activeCount > 0) {
    if (remaining < activeCount) {
      for (let i = 0; i < remaining; i++) {
        active[i].allocation += 1;
        active[i].capacity -= 1;
      }
      return 0;
    }

    let minCapacity = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < activeCount; i++) {
      if (active[i].capacity < minCapacity) {
        minCapacity = active[i].capacity;
      }
    }
    const rounds = Math.min(minCapacity, Math.floor(remaining / activeCount));
    if (rounds <= 0) {
      break;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < activeCount; i++) {
      active[i].allocation += rounds;
      active[i].capacity -= rounds;
    }
    remaining -= rounds * activeCount;

    let writeIndex = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < activeCount; i++) {
      if (active[i].capacity > 0) {
        active[writeIndex++] = active[i];
      }
    }
    activeCount = writeIndex;
  }
  return remaining;
}

function allocateContentTargetsByChildCount(node, totalTarget) {
  const children = node.children.filter((child) => child.count > 0);
  if (children.length === 0) {
    return;
  }

  const minTotal = children.reduce(
    (sum, child) => sum + minimumContentTargetForVirtualBudget(child),
    0,
  );
  const total = Math.max(
    minTotal,
    Math.min(node.count, Math.floor(totalTarget)),
  );
  const allocations = children.map((child) => {
    const minimum = minimumContentTargetForVirtualBudget(child);
    return {
      child,
      allocation: minimum,
      capacity: Math.max(0, child.count - minimum),
      remainder: 0.0,
    };
  });
  let remaining =
    total - allocations.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + entry.allocation, 0);
  if (remaining > 0) {
    const denominator = Math.max(1, node.count);
    for (const entry of allocations) {
      const exact = (remaining * entry.child.count) / denominator;
      const extra = Math.min(entry.capacity, Math.floor(exact));
      entry.allocation += extra;
      entry.capacity -= extra;
      entry.remainder = exact - extra;
    }
    remaining =
      total - allocations.reduce((sum, entry) => sum + entry.allocation, 0);
  }

  remaining = distributeRemainingByPriority(allocations, remaining);

  for (const entry of allocations) {
    entry.child.contentTargetOverride = entry.allocation;
  }
}

function assignVirtualSegmentContentTargetsRecursive(
  node,
  lodMaxDepth,
  samplingRatePerLevel,
) {
  if (node.virtual) {
    const target = resolveVirtualNodeContentTarget(
      node,
      lodMaxDepth,
      samplingRatePerLevel,
    );
    allocateContentTargetsByChildCount(node, target);
  }
  for (const child of node.children) {
    assignVirtualSegmentContentTargetsRecursive(
      child,
      lodMaxDepth,
      samplingRatePerLevel,
    );
  }
}

function assignVirtualSegmentContentTargets(
  node,
  lodMaxDepth,
  samplingRatePerLevel,
) {
  computeVirtualBudgetMinimums(node);
  assignVirtualSegmentContentTargetsRecursive(
    node,
    lodMaxDepth,
    samplingRatePerLevel,
  );
}

module.exports = {
  resolveNodeContentTargetForParams,
  resolveNodeContentTarget,
  assignVirtualSegmentContentTargets,
};
```

### src/tiling/content-writer.js
```
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const { ensure } = require('../formats/parser');
const {
  SPZ_FIXED24_LIMIT,
  SPZ_FRACTIONAL_BITS,
  packCloudToSpz,
} = require('../formats/codec');
const { GltfBuilder } = require('../formats/gltf');
const { computeBounds } = require('./splat-extents');
const {
  computeBucketEntriesBounds,
  writeCanonicalCloudFile,
} = require('../pipeline/bucket-io');
const { serializeBoundsState, deserializeBoundsState } = require('../pipeline/pipeline-state');
const { contentRelPath } = require('../pipeline/pipeline-paths');
const { packBucketEntriesToSpz } = require('./spz-packet-writer');
const { streamSimplifyBucketEntriesExact } = require('./exact-simplify');

const SPZ_BUCKET_ASYNC_WRITE_THRESHOLD = 4096;

function spzBytesPerBucketRow(coeffCount) {
  return 9 + 1 + 3 + 3 + 4 + (coeffCount > 1 ? (coeffCount - 1) * 3 : 0);
}

function safeNodeBoundsTranslation(node) {
  if (!node || !node.bounds) {
    return null;
  }
  const ext = node.bounds.extents();
  const maxLocal = SPZ_FIXED24_LIMIT / (1 << SPZ_FRACTIONAL_BITS);
  if (ext.some((value) => value * 0.5 > maxLocal)) {
    return null;
  }
  return node.bounds.center();
}

function writeCloudGlbOutput(
  outPath,
  cloud,
  colorSpace,
  compressionLevel,
  translation = null,
  sourceCoordinateSystem = null,
) {
  const resolvedTranslation = translation || computeBounds(cloud).center();
  const spzBytes = packCloudToSpz(
    cloud,
    resolvedTranslation,
    { compressionLevel },
  );
  const builder = new GltfBuilder();
  builder.writeSpzStreamGlb(
    outPath,
    spzBytes,
    cloud,
    colorSpace,
    resolvedTranslation,
    sourceCoordinateSystem,
  );
}

function bucketEntriesForWorkerTask(entries) {
  return entries.map((entry) => ({
    filePath: entry.filePath,
    encoding: entry.encoding,
    rowCount: entry.rowCount,
  }));
}

async function writeBucketGlbTaskOutput(task) {
  ensure(task && task.outPath, 'Missing bucket GLB task output path.');
  ensure(task.pointCount > 0, 'Cannot write empty bucket content.');
  const translation =
    task.translation ||
    (
      await computeBucketEntriesBounds(task.entries, task.coeffCount, {
        bucketChunkBytes: task.bucketChunkBytes,
      })
    ).center();
  const spzBytes = await packBucketEntriesToSpz(
    task.entries,
    task.coeffCount,
    task.shDegree,
    translation,
    {
      bucketChunkBytes: task.bucketChunkBytes,
      compressionLevel: task.compressionLevel,
    },
  );
  const builder = new GltfBuilder();
  builder.writeSpzStreamGlb(
    task.outPath,
    spzBytes,
    { length: task.pointCount, shDegree: task.shDegree },
    task.colorSpace,
    translation,
    task.sourceCoordinateSystem,
  );
  return true;
}

async function writeSimplifiedBucketGlbTaskOutput(task) {
  ensure(
    task && task.outPath,
    'Missing simplified bucket GLB task output path.',
  );
  ensure(task.pointCount > 0, 'Cannot simplify an empty bucket content task.');
  const bounds = deserializeBoundsState(task.bounds);
  const { cloud, ownError } = await streamSimplifyBucketEntriesExact(
    task.entries,
    task.coeffCount,
    task.targetCount,
    bounds,
    {
      bucketChunkBytes: task.bucketChunkBytes,
      simplifyScratchBytes: task.simplifyScratchBytes,
      bucketEntryCacheBytes: task.bucketEntryCacheBytes,
      errorBufferBytes: task.errorBufferBytes,
      computeOwnError: task.computeOwnError !== false,
      refineAssignedMedoids: task.refineAssignedMedoids === true,
    },
  );

  const handoffPromise = task.handoffPath
    ? writeCanonicalCloudFile(task.handoffPath, cloud, {
        bucketChunkBytes: task.bucketChunkBytes,
      })
    : Promise.resolve();
  const contentPromise = (async () => {
    await fs.promises.mkdir(path.dirname(task.outPath), { recursive: true });
    writeCloudGlbOutput(
      task.outPath,
      cloud,
      task.colorSpace,
      task.compressionLevel,
      task.translation,
      task.sourceCoordinateSystem,
    );
  })();
  await Promise.all([handoffPromise, contentPromise]);

  return {
    contentUri: task.relPath,
    handoffRowCount: task.handoffPath ? cloud.length : null,
    ownError: Number.isFinite(ownError) && ownError > 0.0 ? ownError : 0.0,
  };
}

async function writeSimplifiedBucketContentFile(
  params,
  entries,
  coeffCount,
  pointCount,
  targetCount,
  bounds,
  level,
  x,
  y,
  z,
  handoffPath,
  options = {},
) {
  ensure(pointCount > 0, 'Cannot write empty simplified bucket content.');
  const relPath = contentRelPath(level, x, y, z);
  const outPath = path.join(params.outputDir, relPath);
  const task = {
    kind: 'simplify-bucket-spz',
    outPath,
    relPath,
    handoffPath,
    entries: bucketEntriesForWorkerTask(entries),
    coeffCount,
    pointCount,
    targetCount,
    bounds: serializeBoundsState(bounds),
    compressionLevel: params.spzCompressionLevel,
    colorSpace: params.colorSpace,
    sourceCoordinateSystem: params.sourceCoordinateSystem,
    translation: options.translation || null,
    bucketChunkBytes: options.bucketChunkBytes,
    simplifyScratchBytes: options.simplifyScratchBytes,
    bucketEntryCacheBytes: options.bucketEntryCacheBytes,
    errorBufferBytes: options.errorBufferBytes,
    computeOwnError: options.computeOwnError !== false,
    refineAssignedMedoids:
      options.refineAssignedMedoids === true ||
      params.refineAssignedMedoids === true,
  };

  if (
    params.contentWorkerPool &&
    pointCount >= SPZ_BUCKET_ASYNC_WRITE_THRESHOLD
  ) {
    return params.contentWorkerPool.submit(task);
  }

  return writeSimplifiedBucketGlbTaskOutput(task);
}

async function writeBucketContentFile(
  params,
  entries,
  coeffCount,
  pointCount,
  shDegree,
  level,
  x,
  y,
  z,
  translation = null,
) {
  ensure(pointCount > 0, 'Cannot write empty bucket content.');
  const relPath = contentRelPath(level, x, y, z);
  const outPath = path.join(params.outputDir, relPath);
  await fs.promises.mkdir(path.dirname(outPath), { recursive: true });

  const task = {
    kind: 'pack-bucket-spz',
    outPath,
    entries: bucketEntriesForWorkerTask(entries),
    coeffCount,
    pointCount,
    shDegree,
    compressionLevel: params.spzCompressionLevel,
    colorSpace: params.colorSpace,
    sourceCoordinateSystem: params.sourceCoordinateSystem,
    translation,
    bucketChunkBytes: params.bucketChunkBytes,
  };

  if (
    params.contentWorkerPool &&
    pointCount >= SPZ_BUCKET_ASYNC_WRITE_THRESHOLD
  ) {
    await params.contentWorkerPool.submit(task);
    return relPath;
  }

  await writeBucketGlbTaskOutput(task);
  return relPath;
}

module.exports = {
  spzBytesPerBucketRow,
  safeNodeBoundsTranslation,
  writeBucketGlbTaskOutput,
  writeSimplifiedBucketGlbTaskOutput,
  writeSimplifiedBucketContentFile,
  writeBucketContentFile,
};
```

### src/tiling/exact-simplify.js
```
const { ensure } = require('../formats/parser');
const { computeBounds } = require('./splat-extents');
const {
  normalizeSplatTargetCount,
  planSimplifyCloudVoxel,
} = require('./simplify-plan');
const {
  makeRowScratch,
  readBucketCoreRowIntoScratch,
  forEachBucketChunk,
  cacheBucketEntriesIfAffordable,
  loadBucketCloudFromEntries,
} = require('../pipeline/bucket-io');
const {
  loadBucketSimplifyCoreFromEntries,
  mergeSelectedBucketRowsToCloud,
  rowRadiusOrScratch,
} = require('./bucket-merge');

const REPRESENTATIVES_PER_VOXEL_TARGET = 24;

class FixedMinHeap {
  constructor(capacity) {
    this.values = new Float32Array(capacity);
    this.length = 0;
    this.capacity = capacity;
  }

  pushCandidate(value) {
    if (this.capacity <= 0) {
      return;
    }
    if (this.length < this.capacity) {
      const index = this.length++;
      this.values[index] = value;
      this._siftUp(index);
      return;
    }
    if (value <= this.values[0]) {
      return;
    }
    this.values[0] = value;
    this._siftDown(0);
  }

  _siftUp(index) {
    const values = this.values;
    const value = values[index];
    while (index > 0) {
      const parent = (index - 1) >> 1;
      const parentValue = values[parent];
      if (parentValue <= value) {
        break;
      }
      values[index] = parentValue;
      index = parent;
    }
    values[index] = value;
  }

  _siftDown(index) {
    const values = this.values;
    const length = this.length;
    const value = values[index];
    while (true) {
      const left = index * 2 + 1;
      if (left >= length) {
        break;
      }
      const right = left + 1;
      let child = left;
      let childValue = values[left];
      if (right < length && values[right] < childValue) {
        child = right;
        childValue = values[right];
      }
      if (childValue >= value) {
        break;
      }
      values[index] = childValue;
      index = child;
    }
    values[index] = value;
  }

  sortedValues() {
    const out = this.values.subarray(0, this.length);
    out.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    return out;
  }
}

function quickselectFloat32(values, kth) {
  let left = 0;
  let right = values.length - 1;
  while (left < right) {
    const mid = (left + right) >> 1;
    const pivot = values[mid];
    let i = left;
    let j = right;

    while (i <= j) {
      while (values[i] < pivot) i++;
      while (values[j] > pivot) j--;
      if (i <= j) {
        const tmp = values[i];
        values[i] = values[j];
        values[j] = tmp;
        i++;
        j--;
      }
    }

    if (kth <= j) {
      right = j;
    } else if (kth >= i) {
      left = i;
    } else {
      return values[kth];
    }
  }
  return values[kth];
}

async function computeExactStreamingOwnErrorFromEntries(
  entries,
  coeffCount,
  assignment,
  outputCloud,
  outputRadius,
  options = {},
) {
  const totalRows = assignment.length;
  const pos95 = 0.95 * (totalRows - 1);
  const lo = Math.floor(pos95);
  const hi = Math.min(totalRows - 1, lo + 1);
  const frac = pos95 - lo;
  const errorBufferBytes = totalRows * Float32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT;
  const inputRadius = options.inputRadius || null;
  if (
    Number.isFinite(options.errorBufferBytes) &&
    errorBufferBytes <= options.errorBufferBytes
  ) {
    const errors = new Float32Array(totalRows);
    const scratch = makeRowScratch(coeffCount);
    let rowIndex = 0;

    await forEachBucketChunk(
      entries,
      coeffCount,
      (chunk) => {
        const {
          view,
          byteOffset,
          rowCount,
          rowByteSize,
          encoding,
          floatView,
          floatBase,
        } = chunk;
        const floatsPerRow = rowByteSize >>> 2;
        for (let row = 0; row < rowCount; row++) {
          readBucketCoreRowIntoScratch(
            encoding,
            view,
            byteOffset + row * rowByteSize,
            scratch,
            floatView,
            floatView ? floatBase + row * floatsPerRow : 0,
          );
          const slot = assignment[rowIndex];
          const dstBase3 = slot * 3;
          const dx = scratch.position[0] - outputCloud.positions[dstBase3 + 0];
          const dy = scratch.position[1] - outputCloud.positions[dstBase3 + 1];
          const dz = scratch.position[2] - outputCloud.positions[dstBase3 + 2];
          const radius = rowRadiusOrScratch(inputRadius, rowIndex, scratch);
          errors[rowIndex] =
            Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) +
            radius +
            outputRadius[slot];
          rowIndex += 1;
        }
      },
      { chunkBytes: options.bucketChunkBytes },
    );

    const loValue = quickselectFloat32(errors, lo);
    if (frac === 0) {
      return loValue;
    }
    const hiValue = quickselectFloat32(errors, hi);
    return loValue * (1 - frac) + hiValue * frac;
  }

  const tailStart = lo;
  const tail = new FixedMinHeap(totalRows - tailStart);
  const scratch = makeRowScratch(coeffCount);
  let rowIndex = 0;

  await forEachBucketChunk(
    entries,
    coeffCount,
    (chunk) => {
      const {
        view,
        byteOffset,
        rowCount,
        rowByteSize,
        encoding,
        floatView,
        floatBase,
      } = chunk;
      const floatsPerRow = rowByteSize >>> 2;
      for (let row = 0; row < rowCount; row++) {
        readBucketCoreRowIntoScratch(
          encoding,
          view,
          byteOffset + row * rowByteSize,
          scratch,
          floatView,
          floatView ? floatBase + row * floatsPerRow : 0,
        );
        const slot = assignment[rowIndex];
        const dstBase3 = slot * 3;
        const dx = scratch.position[0] - outputCloud.positions[dstBase3 + 0];
        const dy = scratch.position[1] - outputCloud.positions[dstBase3 + 1];
        const dz = scratch.position[2] - outputCloud.positions[dstBase3 + 2];
        const radius = rowRadiusOrScratch(inputRadius, rowIndex, scratch);
        const error =
          Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) + radius + outputRadius[slot];
        tail.pushCandidate(error);
        rowIndex += 1;
      }
    },
    { chunkBytes: options.bucketChunkBytes },
  );

  const sortedTail = tail.sortedValues();
  const loValue = sortedTail[0];
  if (frac === 0) {
    return loValue;
  }
  return loValue * (1 - frac) + sortedTail[hi - tailStart] * frac;
}

async function planExactStreamingSimplify(
  entries,
  coeffCount,
  target,
  bounds,
  totalRows,
  {
    bucketChunkBytes = null,
    candidateCacheBytes = null,
    refineAssignedMedoids = false,
    retainInputRadius = false,
  } = {},
) {
  const lightCloud = await loadBucketSimplifyCoreFromEntries(
    entries,
    coeffCount,
    {
      keepScaleQuat: bounds == null,
      keepRgb: true,
      keepCovariance: true,
      bucketChunkBytes,
    },
  );
  const activeBounds = bounds || computeBounds(lightCloud);
  return planSimplifyCloudVoxel(
    lightCloud,
    target,
    activeBounds,
    normalizeSplatTargetCount(
      Math.floor(target / REPRESENTATIVES_PER_VOXEL_TARGET),
      totalRows,
    ),
    {
      candidateCacheBytes,
      refineAssignedMedoids,
      returnOrigRadius: retainInputRadius,
      representativesPerVoxelTarget: REPRESENTATIVES_PER_VOXEL_TARGET,
    },
  );
}

async function streamSimplifyBucketEntriesExact(
  entries,
  coeffCount,
  targetCount,
  bounds,
  options = {},
) {
  const activeEntries = await cacheBucketEntriesIfAffordable(
    entries,
    coeffCount,
    options.bucketEntryCacheBytes,
  );
  const totalRows = activeEntries.reduce(
    (sum, entry) => sum + entry.rowCount,
    0,
  );
  ensure(totalRows > 0, 'Cannot simplify an empty bucket input.');
  const target = normalizeSplatTargetCount(targetCount, totalRows);
  const inputRadiusBytes = totalRows * Float32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT;
  const retainInputRadius =
    options.reuseInputRadius !== false &&
    (!Number.isFinite(options.simplifyScratchBytes) ||
      inputRadiusBytes <= Math.max(0, options.simplifyScratchBytes));
  if (totalRows <= target) {
    return {
      cloud: await loadBucketCloudFromEntries(
        activeEntries,
        coeffCount,
        totalRows,
        { bucketChunkBytes: options.bucketChunkBytes },
      ),
      ownError: 0.0,
    };
  }

  const plan = await planExactStreamingSimplify(
    activeEntries,
    coeffCount,
    target,
    bounds,
    totalRows,
    {
      bucketChunkBytes: options.bucketChunkBytes,
      candidateCacheBytes: options.simplifyScratchBytes,
      refineAssignedMedoids: options.refineAssignedMedoids === true,
      retainInputRadius,
    },
  );
  const selectedRows = plan.selected;
  const selectedCount = selectedRows.length;
  const inputRadius = plan.origRadius || null;
  plan.selected = null;

  const merged = await mergeSelectedBucketRowsToCloud(
    activeEntries,
    coeffCount,
    selectedRows,
    plan.assignment,
    selectedCount,
    plan.voxelDiag,
    { ...options, inputRadius },
  );
  const outputCloud = merged.cloud;
  const outputRadius = merged.outputRadius;
  const computeOwnError = options.computeOwnError !== false;

  return {
    cloud: outputCloud,
    ownError: computeOwnError
      ? await computeExactStreamingOwnErrorFromEntries(
          activeEntries,
          coeffCount,
          plan.assignment,
          outputCloud,
          outputRadius,
          {
            bucketChunkBytes: options.bucketChunkBytes,
            errorBufferBytes: options.errorBufferBytes,
            inputRadius,
          },
        )
      : 0.0,
  };
}

module.exports = {
  streamSimplifyBucketEntriesExact,
};
```

### src/tiling/geometric-error.js
```
const { ConversionError } = require('../formats/parser');

const SOURCE_REPOSITORY = '3DGS-PLY-3DTiles-Converter';

function samplingDivisorForDepth(depth, maxDepth, samplingRatePerLevel) {
  const stepsFromFinest = Math.max(0, Math.floor(maxDepth) - Math.floor(depth));
  return (1.0 / samplingRatePerLevel) ** stepsFromFinest;
}

function geometricErrorScaleForDepth(depth, maxDepth, samplingRatePerLevel) {
  const rootDivisor = samplingDivisorForDepth(
    0,
    maxDepth,
    samplingRatePerLevel,
  );
  const d = samplingDivisorForDepth(depth, maxDepth, samplingRatePerLevel);
  return d / rootDivisor;
}

function rootGeometricErrorFromMinLevel(
  minGeometricError,
  maxDepth,
  samplingRatePerLevel,
) {
  const finestScale = geometricErrorScaleForDepth(
    maxDepth,
    maxDepth,
    samplingRatePerLevel,
  );
  if (!Number.isFinite(finestScale) || finestScale <= 0.0) {
    throw new ConversionError(
      `Invalid geometric error scale at maxDepth=${maxDepth}.`,
    );
  }
  return minGeometricError / finestScale;
}

module.exports = {
  SOURCE_REPOSITORY,
  samplingDivisorForDepth,
  geometricErrorScaleForDepth,
  rootGeometricErrorFromMinLevel,
};
```

### src/tiling/simplify-plan.js
```
const { roundHalfToEven } = require('../formats/parser');
const {
  computeBounds,
  computeThreeSigmaAabbBlendedRadius,
  computeVisualSplatWeights,
} = require('./splat-extents');

const BHATTACHARYYA_COVARIANCE_EPSILON = 1e-8;

function chooseGridDims(bounds, targetCount) {
  const ext = bounds.extents().map((v) => Math.max(v, 1e-6));
  const maxExt = Math.max(ext[0], ext[1], ext[2]);
  const ratios = ext.map((v) => v / maxExt);
  const base = Math.max(1.0, Math.pow(targetCount, 1.0 / 3.0));
  let dims = ratios.map((r) => Math.max(1, roundHalfToEven(base * r)));
  const prod = Math.max(1, dims[0] * dims[1] * dims[2]);
  const scale = Math.pow(targetCount / prod, 1.0 / 3.0);
  dims = dims.map((d) => Math.max(1, roundHalfToEven(d * scale)));
  return dims;
}

function normalizeSplatTargetCount(targetCount, splatCount) {
  return Math.max(1, Math.min(splatCount, Math.floor(targetCount)));
}

function constrainTargetSplatCount(
  targetCount,
  splatCount,
  occupiedChildCount = 0,
) {
  const minCoverage =
    occupiedChildCount > 1
      ? Math.max(1, Math.min(splatCount, occupiedChildCount))
      : 1;
  return Math.max(minCoverage, Math.min(splatCount, Math.floor(targetCount)));
}

function determinantSymmetric3(c00, c01, c02, c11, c12, c22) {
  return (
    c00 * (c11 * c22 - c12 * c12) -
    c01 * (c01 * c22 - c12 * c02) +
    c02 * (c01 * c12 - c11 * c02)
  );
}

function prepareBhattacharyyaTerms(covariance, rowCount, voxelDiagSq) {
  if (!covariance) {
    return null;
  }
  const regularizer = Math.max(
    voxelDiagSq * BHATTACHARYYA_COVARIANCE_EPSILON,
    1e-20,
  );
  const components = new Float64Array(rowCount * 6);
  const logDetQuarter = new Float32Array(rowCount);
  const trace = new Float32Array(rowCount);

  for (let i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    const base = i * 6;
    const c00 = covariance[base + 0] + regularizer;
    const c01 = covariance[base + 1];
    const c02 = covariance[base + 2];
    const c11 = covariance[base + 3] + regularizer;
    const c12 = covariance[base + 4];
    const c22 = covariance[base + 5];
    components[base + 0] = c00;
    components[base + 1] = c01;
    components[base + 2] = c02;
    components[base + 3] = c11;
    components[base + 4] = c12;
    components[base + 5] = c22;
    const determinant = determinantSymmetric3(c00, c01, c02, c11, c12, c22);
    logDetQuarter[i] =
      determinant > 0.0 && Number.isFinite(determinant)
        ? 0.25 * Math.log(determinant)
        : -Infinity;
    trace[i] = c00 + c11 + c22;
  }

  return { components, logDetQuarter, trace };
}

function colorLogSimilarity(rgb, lhs, rhs) {
  if (!rgb) {
    return 0.0;
  }
  return colorLogSimilarityRgb(rgb, lhs, rhs);
}

function colorLogSimilarityRgb(rgb, lhs, rhs) {
  const a = lhs * 3;
  const b = rhs * 3;
  const dr = rgb[a + 0] - rgb[b + 0];
  const dg = rgb[a + 1] - rgb[b + 1];
  const db = rgb[a + 2] - rgb[b + 2];
  return -(dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db);
}

function bhattacharyyaLogCoeff(covarianceTerms, pos, lhs, rhs, voxelDiagSq) {
  if (!covarianceTerms) {
    const lhs3 = lhs * 3;
    const rhs3 = rhs * 3;
    const dx = pos[lhs3 + 0] - pos[rhs3 + 0];
    const dy = pos[lhs3 + 1] - pos[rhs3 + 1];
    const dz = pos[lhs3 + 2] - pos[rhs3 + 2];
    return -(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) / Math.max(voxelDiagSq, 1e-20);
  }
  return bhattacharyyaLogCoeffPrepared(covarianceTerms, pos, lhs, rhs);
}

function bhattacharyyaLogCoeffPrepared(covarianceTerms, pos, lhs, rhs) {
  const covariance = covarianceTerms.components;
  const a = lhs * 6;
  const b = rhs * 6;
  const a00 = covariance[a + 0];
  const a01 = covariance[a + 1];
  const a02 = covariance[a + 2];
  const a11 = covariance[a + 3];
  const a12 = covariance[a + 4];
  const a22 = covariance[a + 5];
  const b00 = covariance[b + 0];
  const b01 = covariance[b + 1];
  const b02 = covariance[b + 2];
  const b11 = covariance[b + 3];
  const b12 = covariance[b + 4];
  const b22 = covariance[b + 5];

  const m00 = 0.5 * (a00 + b00);
  const m01 = 0.5 * (a01 + b01);
  const m02 = 0.5 * (a02 + b02);
  const m11 = 0.5 * (a11 + b11);
  const m12 = 0.5 * (a12 + b12);
  const m22 = 0.5 * (a22 + b22);
  const logDetA = covarianceTerms.logDetQuarter[lhs];
  const logDetB = covarianceTerms.logDetQuarter[rhs];
  if (!Number.isFinite(logDetA) || !Number.isFinite(logDetB)) {
    return -Infinity;
  }
  const detM = determinantSymmetric3(m00, m01, m02, m11, m12, m22);
  if (detM <= 0.0) {
    return -Infinity;
  }

  const invDetM = 1.0 / detM;
  const inv00 = (m11 * m22 - m12 * m12) * invDetM;
  const inv01 = (m02 * m12 - m01 * m22) * invDetM;
  const inv02 = (m01 * m12 - m02 * m11) * invDetM;
  const inv11 = (m00 * m22 - m02 * m02) * invDetM;
  const inv12 = (m01 * m02 - m00 * m12) * invDetM;
  const inv22 = (m00 * m11 - m01 * m01) * invDetM;
  const lhs3 = lhs * 3;
  const rhs3 = rhs * 3;
  const dx = pos[lhs3 + 0] - pos[rhs3 + 0];
  const dy = pos[lhs3 + 1] - pos[rhs3 + 1];
  const dz = pos[lhs3 + 2] - pos[rhs3 + 2];
  const quad =
    dx * (inv00 * dx + inv01 * dy + inv02 * dz) +
    dy * (inv01 * dx + inv11 * dy + inv12 * dz) +
    dz * (inv02 * dx + inv12 * dy + inv22 * dz);
  const logCoefficient =
    logDetA + logDetB - 0.5 * Math.log(detM) - 0.125 * Math.max(quad, 0.0);
  return Number.isFinite(logCoefficient)
    ? Math.min(0.0, logCoefficient)
    : -Infinity;
}

function gaussianLogSimilarity(
  covarianceTerms,
  pos,
  rgb,
  lhs,
  rhs,
  voxelDiagSq,
) {
  return (
    bhattacharyyaLogCoeff(covarianceTerms, pos, lhs, rhs, voxelDiagSq) +
    colorLogSimilarity(rgb, lhs, rhs)
  );
}

function gaussianLogSimilarityIfAbove(
  covarianceTerms,
  pos,
  rgb,
  lhs,
  rhs,
  voxelDiagSq,
  minLogSimilarity,
) {
  const colorLog = colorLogSimilarity(rgb, lhs, rhs);
  if (colorLog < minLogSimilarity) {
    return -Infinity;
  }
  if (covarianceTerms && minLogSimilarity > -Infinity) {
    const lhs3 = lhs * 3;
    const rhs3 = rhs * 3;
    const dx = pos[lhs3 + 0] - pos[rhs3 + 0];
    const dy = pos[lhs3 + 1] - pos[rhs3 + 1];
    const dz = pos[lhs3 + 2] - pos[rhs3 + 2];
    const traceM =
      0.5 * (covarianceTerms.trace[lhs] + covarianceTerms.trace[rhs]);
    if (traceM > 0.0) {
      const upperBound =
        colorLog - (0.125 * (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)) / traceM;
      if (upperBound < minLogSimilarity) {
        return -Infinity;
      }
    }
  }
  return (
    bhattacharyyaLogCoeff(covarianceTerms, pos, lhs, rhs, voxelDiagSq) +
    colorLog
  );
}

function gaussianLogSimilarityRgbCovIfAbove(
  covarianceTerms,
  pos,
  rgb,
  lhs,
  rhs,
  _voxelDiagSq,
  minLogSimilarity,
) {
  const colorLog = colorLogSimilarityRgb(rgb, lhs, rhs);
  if (colorLog < minLogSimilarity) {
    return -Infinity;
  }
  if (minLogSimilarity > -Infinity) {
    const lhs3 = lhs * 3;
    const rhs3 = rhs * 3;
    const dx = pos[lhs3 + 0] - pos[rhs3 + 0];
    const dy = pos[lhs3 + 1] - pos[rhs3 + 1];
    const dz = pos[lhs3 + 2] - pos[rhs3 + 2];
    const traceM =
      0.5 * (covarianceTerms.trace[lhs] + covarianceTerms.trace[rhs]);
    if (traceM > 0.0) {
      const upperBound =
        colorLog - (0.125 * (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)) / traceM;
      if (upperBound < minLogSimilarity) {
        return -Infinity;
      }
    }
  }
  return (
    bhattacharyyaLogCoeffPrepared(covarianceTerms, pos, lhs, rhs) + colorLog
  );
}

function gaussianSimilarity(covarianceTerms, pos, rgb, lhs, rhs, voxelDiagSq) {
  return Math.exp(
    gaussianLogSimilarity(covarianceTerms, pos, rgb, lhs, rhs, voxelDiagSq),
  );
}

function candidatePriorityIsBetter(
  row,
  distance,
  bestRow,
  bestDistance,
  weights,
  radii,
) {
  if (bestRow < 0) {
    return true;
  }
  const weight = weights[row];
  const bestWeight = weights[bestRow];
  const priority = candidateNoveltyPriority(distance, weight);
  const bestPriority = candidateNoveltyPriority(bestDistance, bestWeight);
  if (priority > bestPriority + 1e-12) {
    return true;
  }
  if (Math.abs(priority - bestPriority) > 1e-12) {
    return false;
  }

  if (distance > bestDistance + 1e-12) {
    return true;
  }
  if (Math.abs(distance - bestDistance) > 1e-12) {
    return false;
  }

  const weightDiff = weight - bestWeight;
  if (Math.abs(weightDiff) > 1e-12) {
    return weightDiff > 0.0;
  }

  const radiusDiff = radii[row] - radii[bestRow];
  if (Math.abs(radiusDiff) > 1e-12) {
    return radiusDiff > 0.0;
  }
  return row > bestRow;
}

function candidateNoveltyPriority(distance, weight) {
  return distance * Math.max(weight, 1e-12);
}

const CANDIDATE_CACHE_BYTES_PER_ROW =
  Int32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT + Float32Array.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT;

function candidateDistanceFromLog(logSimilarity) {
  return logSimilarity === -Infinity ? Infinity : Math.max(0.0, -logSimilarity);
}

function candidateHeapEntryIsLess(
  lhsPriority,
  lhsWeight,
  lhsDistance,
  lhsRadius,
  lhsRow,
  lhsGroup,
  rhsPriority,
  rhsWeight,
  rhsDistance,
  rhsRadius,
  rhsRow,
  rhsGroup,
) {
  if (lhsPriority !== rhsPriority) {
    return lhsPriority > rhsPriority;
  }
  if (lhsWeight !== rhsWeight) {
    return lhsWeight > rhsWeight;
  }
  if (lhsDistance !== rhsDistance) {
    return lhsDistance > rhsDistance;
  }
  if (lhsRadius !== rhsRadius) {
    return lhsRadius > rhsRadius;
  }
  if (lhsRow !== rhsRow) {
    return lhsRow > rhsRow;
  }
  return lhsGroup < rhsGroup;
}

class GroupCandidateHeap {
  constructor(capacity = 16) {
    this.capacity = Math.max(1, Math.floor(capacity));
    this.groups = new Int32Array(this.capacity);
    this.versions = new Uint32Array(this.capacity);
    this.rows = new Int32Array(this.capacity);
    this.distances = new Float64Array(this.capacity);
    this.weights = new Float64Array(this.capacity);
    this.priorities = new Float64Array(this.capacity);
    this.radii = new Float64Array(this.capacity);
    this.length = 0;
  }

  _grow() {
    const nextCapacity = this.capacity * 2;
    const nextGroups = new Int32Array(nextCapacity);
    const nextVersions = new Uint32Array(nextCapacity);
    const nextRows = new Int32Array(nextCapacity);
    const nextDistances = new Float64Array(nextCapacity);
    const nextWeights = new Float64Array(nextCapacity);
    const nextPriorities = new Float64Array(nextCapacity);
    const nextRadii = new Float64Array(nextCapacity);
    nextGroups.set(this.groups);
    nextVersions.set(this.versions);
    nextRows.set(this.rows);
    nextDistances.set(this.distances);
    nextWeights.set(this.weights);
    nextPriorities.set(this.priorities);
    nextRadii.set(this.radii);
    this.capacity = nextCapacity;
    this.groups = nextGroups;
    this.versions = nextVersions;
    this.rows = nextRows;
    this.distances = nextDistances;
    this.weights = nextWeights;
    this.priorities = nextPriorities;
    this.radii = nextRadii;
  }

  _entryAtIsLess(index, priority, weight, distance, radius, row, group) {
    return candidateHeapEntryIsLess(
      this.priorities[index],
      this.weights[index],
      this.distances[index],
      this.radii[index],
      this.rows[index],
      this.groups[index],
      priority,
      weight,
      distance,
      radius,
      row,
      group,
    );
  }

  _valueIsLessThanIndex(priority, weight, distance, radius, row, group, index) {
    return candidateHeapEntryIsLess(
      priority,
      weight,
      distance,
      radius,
      row,
      group,
      this.priorities[index],
      this.weights[index],
      this.distances[index],
      this.radii[index],
      this.rows[index],
      this.groups[index],
    );
  }

  _set(index, group, version, row, distance, weight, priority, radius) {
    this.groups[index] = group;
    this.versions[index] = version;
    this.rows[index] = row;
    this.distances[index] = distance;
    this.weights[index] = weight;
    this.priorities[index] = priority;
    this.radii[index] = radius;
  }

  push(group, version, row, distance, weight, priority, radius) {
    if (this.length >= this.capacity) {
      this._grow();
    }
    let index = this.length++;
    while (index > 0) {
      const parent = (index - 1) >> 1;
      if (
        !this._valueIsLessThanIndex(
          priority,
          weight,
          distance,
          radius,
          row,
          group,
          parent,
        )
      ) {
        break;
      }
      this._set(
        index,
        this.groups[parent],
        this.versions[parent],
        this.rows[parent],
        this.distances[parent],
        this.weights[parent],
        this.priorities[parent],
        this.radii[parent],
      );
      index = parent;
    }
    this._set(index, group, version, row, distance, weight, priority, radius);
  }

  pop(out) {
    if (this.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }

    out.group = this.groups[0];
    out.version = this.versions[0];
    out.row = this.rows[0];
    out.distance = this.distances[0];
    out.weight = this.weights[0];
    out.priority = this.priorities[0];
    out.radius = this.radii[0];

    const lastIndex = --this.length;
    if (lastIndex === 0) {
      return true;
    }

    const group = this.groups[lastIndex];
    const version = this.versions[lastIndex];
    const row = this.rows[lastIndex];
    const distance = this.distances[lastIndex];
    const weight = this.weights[lastIndex];
    const priority = this.priorities[lastIndex];
    const radius = this.radii[lastIndex];

    let index = 0;
    while (true) {
      const left = index * 2 + 1;
      if (left >= this.length) {
        break;
      }
      const right = left + 1;
      let child = left;
      if (
        right < this.length &&
        candidateHeapEntryIsLess(
          this.priorities[right],
          this.weights[right],
          this.distances[right],
          this.radii[right],
          this.rows[right],
          this.groups[right],
          this.priorities[left],
          this.weights[left],
          this.distances[left],
          this.radii[left],
          this.rows[left],
          this.groups[left],
        )
      ) {
        child = right;
      }
      if (
        !this._entryAtIsLess(
          child,
          priority,
          weight,
          distance,
          radius,
          row,
          group,
        )
      ) {
        break;
      }
      this._set(
        index,
        this.groups[child],
        this.versions[child],
        this.rows[child],
        this.distances[child],
        this.weights[child],
        this.priorities[child],
        this.radii[child],
      );
      index = child;
    }
    this._set(index, group, version, row, distance, weight, priority, radius);
    return true;
  }
}

function representativeIndexForGroupRange(
  cloud,
  groupIndices,
  start,
  end,
  weightAt,
  radii,
  voxelDiagSq,
) {
  if (end <= start) {
    return -1;
  }

  const pos = cloud.positions;
  let fallback = -1;
  let fallbackWeight = -Infinity;
  let fallbackRadius = -Infinity;
  let validCount = 0;
  let totalWeight = 0.0;

  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
    const idx = groupIndices[p];
    validCount += 1;
    const rawWeight = weightAt(idx);
    const w = Number.isFinite(rawWeight) ? Math.max(rawWeight, 1e-12) : 1e-12;
    const radius = Number.isFinite(radii[idx]) ? radii[idx] : -Infinity;
    totalWeight += w;
    if (
      fallback < 0 ||
      w > fallbackWeight + 1e-12 ||
      (Math.abs(w - fallbackWeight) <= 1e-12 &&
        (radius > fallbackRadius + 1e-12 ||
          (Math.abs(radius - fallbackRadius) <= 1e-12 && idx > fallback)))
    ) {
      fallback = idx;
      fallbackWeight = w;
      fallbackRadius = radius;
    }
  }

  if (validCount <= 0 || fallback < 0) {
    return -1;
  }
  if (validCount === 1) {
    return fallback;
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(totalWeight) || totalWeight <= 0.0) {
    return fallback;
  }

  let rep = fallback;
  let bestCost = Infinity;
  let bestMedoidCost = Infinity;
  let bestWeight = fallbackWeight;
  let bestRadius = fallbackRadius;
  const invVoxelDiagSq = 1.0 / Math.max(voxelDiagSq, 1e-12);
  const invTotalWeight = 1.0 / totalWeight;
  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
    const idx = groupIndices[p];
    const i3 = idx * 3;
    const radius = Number.isFinite(radii[idx]) ? radii[idx] : -Infinity;
    const rawWeight = weightAt(idx);
    const w = Number.isFinite(rawWeight) ? Math.max(rawWeight, 1e-12) : 1e-12;
    let cost = 0.0;
    for (let q = start; q < end; q++) {
      const other = groupIndices[q];
      const j3 = other * 3;
      const dx = pos[i3 + 0] - pos[j3 + 0];
      const dy = pos[i3 + 1] - pos[j3 + 1];
      const dz = pos[i3 + 2] - pos[j3 + 2];
      const otherRawWeight = weightAt(other);
      const otherWeight = Number.isFinite(otherRawWeight)
        ? Math.max(otherRawWeight, 1e-12)
        : 1e-12;
      cost += otherWeight * (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * invVoxelDiagSq;
      if ((cost * invTotalWeight) / w > bestCost + 1e-12) {
        break;
      }
    }
    const medoidCost = cost * invTotalWeight;
    const weightedCost = medoidCost / w;
    if (
      weightedCost < bestCost - 1e-12 ||
      (Math.abs(weightedCost - bestCost) <= 1e-12 &&
        (medoidCost < bestMedoidCost - 1e-12 ||
          (Math.abs(medoidCost - bestMedoidCost) <= 1e-12 &&
            (w > bestWeight + 1e-12 ||
              (Math.abs(w - bestWeight) <= 1e-12 &&
                (radius > bestRadius + 1e-12 ||
                  (Math.abs(radius - bestRadius) <= 1e-12 && idx > rep)))))))
    ) {
      rep = idx;
      bestCost = weightedCost;
      bestMedoidCost = medoidCost;
      bestWeight = w;
      bestRadius = radius;
    }
  }
  return rep;
}

function computeAdaptiveMaxRepsByGroup(
  cloud,
  groupIndices,
  groupOffsets,
  target,
  representativesPerVoxelTarget,
  weights,
  voxelDiagSq,
) {
  const groupCount = groupOffsets.length - 1;
  const maxRepsByGroup = new Uint32Array(groupCount);
  if (groupCount <= 0) {
    return maxRepsByGroup;
  }

  const pos = cloud.positions;
  const rgb = cloud.rgb || null;
  const covariance = cloud.covariance || null;
  const invVoxelDiagSq = 1.0 / Math.max(voxelDiagSq, 1e-12);
  const capacities = new Uint32Array(groupCount);
  const complexity = new Float64Array(groupCount);
  let baseBudget = 0;
  let capacityTotal = 0;

  for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
    const start = groupOffsets[group];
    const end = groupOffsets[group + 1];
    const size = end - start;
    if (size <= 0) {
      continue;
    }

    maxRepsByGroup[group] = 1;
    baseBudget += 1;
    const capacity = size - 1;
    capacities[group] = capacity;
    capacityTotal += capacity;
    if (capacity <= 0) {
      continue;
    }

    let sumW = 0.0;
    let meanX = 0.0;
    let meanY = 0.0;
    let meanZ = 0.0;
    let meanR = 0.0;
    let meanG = 0.0;
    let meanB = 0.0;
    let covarianceTrace = 0.0;
    for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
      const row = groupIndices[p];
      const weight = Number.isFinite(weights[row])
        ? Math.max(weights[row], 1e-12)
        : 1e-12;
      const base3 = row * 3;
      sumW += weight;
      meanX += pos[base3 + 0] * weight;
      meanY += pos[base3 + 1] * weight;
      meanZ += pos[base3 + 2] * weight;
      if (rgb) {
        meanR += rgb[base3 + 0] * weight;
        meanG += rgb[base3 + 1] * weight;
        meanB += rgb[base3 + 2] * weight;
      }
      if (covariance) {
        const base6 = row * 6;
        const trace =
          covariance[base6 + 0] + covariance[base6 + 3] + covariance[base6 + 5];
        if (Number.isFinite(trace) && trace > 0.0) {
          covarianceTrace += weight * trace * invVoxelDiagSq;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(sumW) || sumW <= 0.0) {
      continue;
    }

    const invSumW = 1.0 / sumW;
    meanX *= invSumW;
    meanY *= invSumW;
    meanZ *= invSumW;
    if (rgb) {
      meanR *= invSumW;
      meanG *= invSumW;
      meanB *= invSumW;
    }

    let positionVariance = 0.0;
    let colorVariance = 0.0;
    for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
      const row = groupIndices[p];
      const weight = Number.isFinite(weights[row])
        ? Math.max(weights[row], 1e-12)
        : 1e-12;
      const base3 = row * 3;
      const dx = pos[base3 + 0] - meanX;
      const dy = pos[base3 + 1] - meanY;
      const dz = pos[base3 + 2] - meanZ;
      positionVariance +=
        weight * (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * invVoxelDiagSq;
      if (rgb) {
        const dr = rgb[base3 + 0] - meanR;
        const dg = rgb[base3 + 1] - meanG;
        const db = rgb[base3 + 2] - meanB;
        colorVariance += weight * (dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db);
      }
    }

    const score = positionVariance + colorVariance + covarianceTrace;
    complexity[group] = Number.isFinite(score) && score > 0.0 ? score : 0.0;
  }

  const averageBudget = Math.max(
    baseBudget,
    groupCount * representativesPerVoxelTarget,
  );
  const localBudget = Math.min(
    target,
    baseBudget + capacityTotal,
    averageBudget,
  );
  let remaining = Math.max(0, localBudget - baseBudget);
  if (remaining <= 0 || capacityTotal <= 0) {
    return maxRepsByGroup;
  }

  let totalScore = 0.0;
  for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
    if (capacities[group] > 0) {
      totalScore += complexity[group];
    }
  }
  const useCapacityFallback = !Number.isFinite(totalScore) || totalScore <= 0.0;
  if (useCapacityFallback) {
    totalScore = capacityTotal;
  }

  const assignedExtras = new Uint32Array(groupCount);
  const exactExtras = new Float64Array(groupCount);
  const order = [];
  let allocated = 0;
  for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
    const capacity = capacities[group];
    if (capacity <= 0) {
      continue;
    }
    const score = useCapacityFallback ? capacity : complexity[group];
    if (!Number.isFinite(score) || score <= 0.0) {
      continue;
    }

    const exact = (remaining * score) / totalScore;
    const extra = Math.min(capacity, Math.floor(exact));
    exactExtras[group] = exact;
    assignedExtras[group] = extra;
    maxRepsByGroup[group] += extra;
    allocated += extra;
    if (extra < capacity) {
      order.push(group);
    }
  }

  remaining -= allocated;
  order.sort((a, b) => {
    const aRemainder = exactExtras[a] - assignedExtras[a];
    const bRemainder = exactExtras[b] - assignedExtras[b];
    if (aRemainder !== bRemainder) {
      return bRemainder - aRemainder;
    }
    const aScore = useCapacityFallback ? capacities[a] : complexity[a];
    const bScore = useCapacityFallback ? capacities[b] : complexity[b];
    if (aScore !== bScore) {
      return bScore - aScore;
    }
    const aCapacity = capacities[a] - assignedExtras[a];
    const bCapacity = capacities[b] - assignedExtras[b];
    if (aCapacity !== bCapacity) {
      return bCapacity - aCapacity;
    }
    return a - b;
  });

  while (remaining > 0 && order.length > 0) {
    let wrote = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < order.length && remaining > 0; i++) {
      const group = order[i];
      if (assignedExtras[group] >= capacities[group]) {
        continue;
      }
      assignedExtras[group] += 1;
      maxRepsByGroup[group] += 1;
      remaining -= 1;
      wrote += 1;
    }
    if (wrote === 0) {
      break;
    }
  }

  return maxRepsByGroup;
}

function planSimplifyCloudVoxel(
  cloud,
  targetCount,
  bounds = null,
  voxelTargetCount = targetCount,
  options = {},
) {
  const returnOrigRadius = options.returnOrigRadius !== false;
  const refineAssignedMedoids = options.refineAssignedMedoids === true;
  const n = cloud.length;
  const target = normalizeSplatTargetCount(targetCount, n);
  const voxelTarget = normalizeSplatTargetCount(voxelTargetCount, n);
  const representativesPerVoxelTarget = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.floor(options.representativesPerVoxelTarget || 4),
  );
  if (n <= target) {
    const selected = [];
    const assignment = new Int32Array(n);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      selected.push(i);
      assignment[i] = i;
    }
    const origRadius =
      cloud.origRadius ||
      computeThreeSigmaAabbBlendedRadius(cloud.scaleLog, cloud.quatsXYZW);
    return {
      selected,
      assignment,
      origRadius: returnOrigRadius ? origRadius : null,
      voxelDiag: 0.0,
    };
  }

  const activeBounds = bounds || computeBounds(cloud);
  let dims = chooseGridDims(activeBounds, voxelTarget);
  const mins = activeBounds.minimum;
  const ext = activeBounds.extents().map((v) => Math.max(v, 1e-6));

  const pos = cloud.positions;
  const m0 = mins[0];
  const m1 = mins[1];
  const m2 = mins[2];
  const invExt0 = 1.0 / ext[0];
  const invExt1 = 1.0 / ext[1];
  const invExt2 = 1.0 / ext[2];
  let groupKeys = [];
  let cellCounts = null;
  const pointFlats = new Int32Array(n);

  const flatForPoint = (idx, dimsIn) => {
    const i3 = idx * 3;
    const d0 = dimsIn[0];
    const d1 = dimsIn[1];
    const d2 = dimsIn[2];
    const uvw0 = Math.max(0.0, Math.min(0.999999, (pos[i3] - m0) * invExt0));
    const uvw1 = Math.max(
      0.0,
      Math.min(0.999999, (pos[i3 + 1] - m1) * invExt1),
    );
    const uvw2 = Math.max(
      0.0,
      Math.min(0.999999, (pos[i3 + 2] - m2) * invExt2),
    );
    const iIdx = Math.min(d0 - 1, Math.floor(uvw0 * d0));
    const jIdx = Math.min(d1 - 1, Math.floor(uvw1 * d1));
    const kIdx = Math.min(d2 - 1, Math.floor(uvw2 * d2));
    return iIdx + d0 * (jIdx + d1 * kIdx);
  };

  for (let iter = 0; iter < 24; iter++) {
    const cellCount = dims[0] * dims[1] * dims[2];
    cellCounts = new Uint32Array(cellCount);

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const flat = flatForPoint(i, dims);
      pointFlats[i] = flat;
      cellCounts[flat] += 1;
    }

    groupKeys = [];
    for (let flat = 0; flat < cellCount; flat++) {
      if (cellCounts[flat] > 0) {
        groupKeys.push(flat);
      }
    }
    if (
      groupKeys.length <= voxelTarget ||
      (dims[0] === 1 && dims[1] === 1 && dims[2] === 1)
    ) {
      break;
    }

    dims = [
      Math.max(1, Math.floor(dims[0] * 0.85)),
      Math.max(1, Math.floor(dims[1] * 0.85)),
      Math.max(1, Math.floor(dims[2] * 0.85)),
    ];
  }

  const groupCount = groupKeys.length;
  const groupForFlat = new Int32Array(cellCounts.length);
  groupForFlat.fill(-1);
  const groupOffsets = new Uint32Array(groupCount + 1);
  for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
    const key = groupKeys[group];
    groupOffsets[group + 1] = groupOffsets[group] + cellCounts[key];
    groupForFlat[key] = group;
  }
  const groupIndices = new Int32Array(n);
  const groupCursors = new Uint32Array(groupOffsets);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const group = groupForFlat[pointFlats[i]];
    groupIndices[groupCursors[group]++] = i;
  }
  cellCounts = null;

  const neighborGroupsByGroup = new Array(groupCount);
  const d0 = dims[0];
  const d1 = dims[1];
  const d2 = dims[2];
  for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
    const key = groupKeys[group];
    const gx = key % d0;
    const gy = Math.floor(key / d0) % d1;
    const gz = Math.floor(key / (d0 * d1));
    const neighborGroups = [group];
    for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {
      const nz = gz + dz;
      if (nz < 0 || nz >= d2) {
        continue;
      }
      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
        const ny = gy + dy;
        if (ny < 0 || ny >= d1) {
          continue;
        }
        for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
          if (dx === 0 && dy === 0 && dz === 0) {
            continue;
          }
          const nx = gx + dx;
          if (nx < 0 || nx >= d0) {
            continue;
          }
          const neighborFlat = nx + d0 * (ny + d1 * nz);
          const neighborGroup = groupForFlat[neighborFlat];
          if (neighborGroup >= 0) {
            neighborGroups.push(neighborGroup);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    neighborGroupsByGroup[group] = neighborGroups;
  }
  groupKeys = [];

  const origRadius =
    cloud.origRadius ||
    computeThreeSigmaAabbBlendedRadius(cloud.scaleLog, cloud.quatsXYZW);
  const visualWeights =
    cloud.visualWeights ||
    (cloud.scaleLog && cloud.quatsXYZW
      ? computeVisualSplatWeights(
          cloud.scaleLog,
          cloud.quatsXYZW,
          cloud.opacity,
        )
      : null);
  const voxelSize0 = ext[0] / Math.max(1, dims[0]);
  const voxelSize1 = ext[1] / Math.max(1, dims[1]);
  const voxelSize2 = ext[2] / Math.max(1, dims[2]);
  const voxelDiagSq =
    voxelSize0 * voxelSize0 + voxelSize1 * voxelSize1 + voxelSize2 * voxelSize2;
  const voxelDiag = Math.max(Math.sqrt(voxelDiagSq), 1e-6);
  const coarseWeightAt = (i) => {
    if (visualWeights) {
      return Math.max(visualWeights[i], 1e-12);
    }
    const opacity = Math.max(cloud.opacity[i], 1e-4);
    const radiusNorm = Math.max(origRadius[i] / voxelDiag, 0.35);
    return opacity * Math.sqrt(radiusNorm);
  };
  const coarseWeights = new Float32Array(n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    coarseWeights[i] = coarseWeightAt(i);
  }
  const cachedCoarseWeightAt = (i) => coarseWeights[i];

  const selected = [];
  const assignment = new Int32Array(n);
  assignment.fill(-1);
  const groupReps = new Array(groupCount);
  const rgb = cloud.rgb || null;
  const covariance = cloud.covariance || null;
  let covarianceTerms = null;
  let covarianceTermsPrepared = false;
  const getCovarianceTerms = () => {
    if (!covarianceTermsPrepared) {
      covarianceTerms = prepareBhattacharyyaTerms(covariance, n, voxelDiagSq);
      covarianceTermsPrepared = true;
    }
    return covarianceTerms;
  };
  const maxGroupRepLogSimilarity = (
    group,
    row,
    activeCovarianceTerms,
    similarityIfAbove,
  ) => {
    const reps = groupReps[group] || [];
    let maxLogSimilarity = -Infinity;
    for (let r = 0; r < reps.length; r++) {
      const rep = selected[reps[r]];
      const logSimilarity = similarityIfAbove(
        activeCovarianceTerms,
        pos,
        rgb,
        row,
        rep,
        voxelDiagSq,
        maxLogSimilarity,
      );
      if (logSimilarity > maxLogSimilarity) {
        maxLogSimilarity = logSimilarity;
        if (maxLogSimilarity >= 0.0) {
          return maxLogSimilarity;
        }
      }
    }
    return maxLogSimilarity;
  };

  for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
    const start = groupOffsets[group];
    const end = groupOffsets[group + 1];
    if (end <= start) {
      continue;
    }
    const coarseRep = representativeIndexForGroupRange(
      cloud,
      groupIndices,
      start,
      end,
      cachedCoarseWeightAt,
      origRadius,
      voxelDiagSq,
    );
    const coarseOutIdx = selected.length;
    selected.push(coarseRep);
    assignment[coarseRep] = coarseOutIdx;
    groupReps[group] = [coarseOutIdx];
  }

  const maxRepsByGroup = computeAdaptiveMaxRepsByGroup(
    cloud,
    groupIndices,
    groupOffsets,
    target,
    representativesPerVoxelTarget,
    coarseWeights,
    voxelDiagSq,
  );

  const candidateCacheBytes = Number.isFinite(options.candidateCacheBytes)
    ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(options.candidateCacheBytes))
    : Infinity;
  let repSelectionCovarianceTerms = null;
  let repSelectionSimilarityIfAbove = null;
  const ensureRepSelectionContext = () => {
    if (!repSelectionSimilarityIfAbove) {
      repSelectionCovarianceTerms = getCovarianceTerms();
      repSelectionSimilarityIfAbove =
        repSelectionCovarianceTerms && rgb
          ? gaussianLogSimilarityRgbCovIfAbove
          : gaussianLogSimilarityIfAbove;
    }
    return {
      activeCovarianceTerms: repSelectionCovarianceTerms,
      similarityIfAbove: repSelectionSimilarityIfAbove,
    };
  };

  const createCandidateState = (activeCovarianceTerms, similarityIfAbove) => {
    const candidateCapacityByGroup = new Uint32Array(groupCount);
    let totalCandidateCapacity = 0;
    let cacheableGroupCount = 0;
    for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
      const size = groupOffsets[group + 1] - groupOffsets[group];
      const reps = groupReps[group];
      const selectedInGroup = reps ? reps.length : 0;
      const capacity = Math.max(0, size - selectedInGroup);
      candidateCapacityByGroup[group] = capacity;
      totalCandidateCapacity += capacity;
      if (capacity > 0) {
        cacheableGroupCount += 1;
      }
    }

    const cachedGroups = new Uint8Array(groupCount);
    let cachedCandidateCapacity = 0;
    const fullCacheBytes =
      totalCandidateCapacity * CANDIDATE_CACHE_BYTES_PER_ROW;
    if (
      totalCandidateCapacity > 0 &&
      candidateCacheBytes >= CANDIDATE_CACHE_BYTES_PER_ROW
    ) {
      if (
        !Number.isFinite(candidateCacheBytes) ||
        fullCacheBytes <= candidateCacheBytes
      ) {
        for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
          const capacity = candidateCapacityByGroup[group];
          if (capacity > 0) {
            cachedGroups[group] = 1;
            cachedCandidateCapacity += capacity;
          }
        }
      } else if (cacheableGroupCount > 0) {
        const groupOrder = new Uint32Array(cacheableGroupCount);
        let write = 0;
        for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
          if (candidateCapacityByGroup[group] > 0) {
            groupOrder[write++] = group;
          }
        }
        groupOrder.sort((a, b) => {
          const aSize = groupOffsets[a + 1] - groupOffsets[a];
          const bSize = groupOffsets[b + 1] - groupOffsets[b];
          const aBenefit = aSize * maxRepsByGroup[a];
          const bBenefit = bSize * maxRepsByGroup[b];
          if (aBenefit !== bBenefit) {
            return bBenefit - aBenefit;
          }
          const aCapacity = candidateCapacityByGroup[a];
          const bCapacity = candidateCapacityByGroup[b];
          if (aCapacity !== bCapacity) {
            return bCapacity - aCapacity;
          }
          return a - b;
        });

        let remainingBytes = candidateCacheBytes;
        for (let i = 0; i < groupOrder.length; i++) {
          const group = groupOrder[i];
          const capacity = candidateCapacityByGroup[group];
          const bytes = capacity * CANDIDATE_CACHE_BYTES_PER_ROW;
          if (bytes <= remainingBytes) {
            cachedGroups[group] = 1;
            cachedCandidateCapacity += capacity;
            remainingBytes -= bytes;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const candidateStarts =
      cachedCandidateCapacity > 0 ? new Uint32Array(groupCount + 1) : null;
    const candidateLengths =
      cachedCandidateCapacity > 0 ? new Uint32Array(groupCount) : null;
    const groupBestIndex =
      cachedCandidateCapacity > 0 ? new Int32Array(groupCount) : null;
    if (groupBestIndex) {
      groupBestIndex.fill(-1);
    }

    if (candidateStarts) {
      let offset = 0;
      for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
        candidateStarts[group] = offset;
        if (cachedGroups[group]) {
          offset += candidateCapacityByGroup[group];
        }
      }
      candidateStarts[groupCount] = offset;
    }

    const candidateRows =
      cachedCandidateCapacity > 0
        ? new Int32Array(cachedCandidateCapacity)
        : null;
    const candidateLogSimilarities =
      cachedCandidateCapacity > 0
        ? new Float32Array(cachedCandidateCapacity)
        : null;

    if (candidateRows) {
      for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
        if (!cachedGroups[group]) {
          continue;
        }
        const start = groupOffsets[group];
        const end = groupOffsets[group + 1];
        const slotStart = candidateStarts[group];
        let length = 0;
        for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
          const row = groupIndices[p];
          if (assignment[row] >= 0) {
            continue;
          }
          const maxLogSimilarity = maxGroupRepLogSimilarity(
            group,
            row,
            activeCovarianceTerms,
            similarityIfAbove,
          );
          const slot = slotStart + length;
          candidateRows[slot] = row;
          candidateLogSimilarities[slot] = maxLogSimilarity;
          length += 1;
        }
        candidateLengths[group] = length;
      }
    }

    const findCachedGroupBest = (group, out) => {
      const length = candidateLengths[group];
      if (length === 0) {
        groupBestIndex[group] = -1;
        return false;
      }
      const slotStart = candidateStarts[group];
      let bestIndex = -1;
      let bestRow = -1;
      let bestDistance = -Infinity;
      for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        const slot = slotStart + i;
        const row = candidateRows[slot];
        const distance = candidateDistanceFromLog(
          candidateLogSimilarities[slot],
        );
        if (
          candidatePriorityIsBetter(
            row,
            distance,
            bestRow,
            bestDistance,
            coarseWeights,
            origRadius,
          )
        ) {
          bestIndex = i;
          bestRow = row;
          bestDistance = distance;
        }
      }
      if (bestRow < 0) {
        groupBestIndex[group] = -1;
        return false;
      }
      groupBestIndex[group] = bestIndex;
      out.row = bestRow;
      out.distance = bestDistance;
      out.cached = true;
      return true;
    };

    const findScannedGroupBest = (group, out) => {
      const start = groupOffsets[group];
      const end = groupOffsets[group + 1];
      let bestRow = -1;
      let bestDistance = -Infinity;
      for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
        const row = groupIndices[p];
        if (assignment[row] >= 0) {
          continue;
        }
        const maxLogSimilarity = maxGroupRepLogSimilarity(
          group,
          row,
          activeCovarianceTerms,
          similarityIfAbove,
        );
        const distance = candidateDistanceFromLog(maxLogSimilarity);
        if (
          candidatePriorityIsBetter(
            row,
            distance,
            bestRow,
            bestDistance,
            coarseWeights,
            origRadius,
          )
        ) {
          bestRow = row;
          bestDistance = distance;
        }
      }
      if (bestRow < 0) {
        return false;
      }
      out.row = bestRow;
      out.distance = bestDistance;
      out.cached = false;
      return true;
    };

    const findGroupBest = (group, out) =>
      cachedGroups[group]
        ? findCachedGroupBest(group, out)
        : findScannedGroupBest(group, out);

    const removeSelectedCandidate = (group) => {
      if (!cachedGroups[group]) {
        return;
      }
      const length = candidateLengths[group];
      const bestIndex = groupBestIndex[group];
      if (bestIndex < 0 || bestIndex >= length) {
        return;
      }
      const slotStart = candidateStarts[group];
      const last = length - 1;
      if (bestIndex !== last) {
        const dst = slotStart + bestIndex;
        const src = slotStart + last;
        candidateRows[dst] = candidateRows[src];
        candidateLogSimilarities[dst] = candidateLogSimilarities[src];
      }
      candidateLengths[group] = last;
      groupBestIndex[group] = -1;
    };

    const updateCachedSimilaritiesForGroup = (group, rep) => {
      if (!cachedGroups[group]) {
        return;
      }
      const length = candidateLengths[group];
      if (length === 0) {
        return;
      }
      const slotStart = candidateStarts[group];
      for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
        const slot = slotStart + i;
        if (candidateLogSimilarities[slot] >= 0.0) {
          continue;
        }
        const logSimilarity = similarityIfAbove(
          activeCovarianceTerms,
          pos,
          rgb,
          candidateRows[slot],
          rep,
          voxelDiagSq,
          candidateLogSimilarities[slot],
        );
        if (logSimilarity > candidateLogSimilarities[slot]) {
          candidateLogSimilarities[slot] = logSimilarity;
        }
      }
    };

    return {
      findGroupBest,
      removeSelectedCandidate,
      updateCachedSimilaritiesForGroup,
    };
  };

  let candidateState = null;
  const ensureCandidateState = () => {
    if (!candidateState) {
      const { activeCovarianceTerms, similarityIfAbove } =
        ensureRepSelectionContext();
      candidateState = createCandidateState(
        activeCovarianceTerms,
        similarityIfAbove,
      );
    }
    return candidateState;
  };

  if (representativesPerVoxelTarget > 1 && selected.length < target) {
    const state = ensureCandidateState();
    const best = {};
    for (
      let group = 0;
      group < groupCount && selected.length < target;
      group++
    ) {
      const reps = groupReps[group] || [];
      const maxReps = maxRepsByGroup[group];
      if (reps.length >= maxReps) {
        groupReps[group] = reps;
        continue;
      }
      while (reps.length < maxReps && selected.length < target) {
        if (!state.findGroupBest(group, best)) {
          break;
        }
        const bestRow = best.row;
        const outIdx = selected.length;
        selected.push(bestRow);
        assignment[bestRow] = outIdx;
        reps.push(outIdx);
        state.removeSelectedCandidate(group);
        state.updateCachedSimilaritiesForGroup(group, bestRow);
      }
      groupReps[group] = reps;
    }
  }

  if (selected.length < target) {
    const state = ensureCandidateState();
    const groupVersions = new Uint32Array(groupCount);
    const heap = new GroupCandidateHeap(target + groupCount + 1);
    const heapEntry = {};
    const best = {};

    const pushGroupBest = (group) => {
      if (!state.findGroupBest(group, best)) {
        return;
      }
      const version = ++groupVersions[group];
      const weight = coarseWeights[best.row];
      heap.push(
        group,
        version,
        best.row,
        best.distance,
        weight,
        candidateNoveltyPriority(best.distance, weight),
        origRadius[best.row],
      );
    };

    for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
      pushGroupBest(group);
    }

    while (selected.length < target && heap.length > 0) {
      if (!heap.pop(heapEntry)) {
        break;
      }
      if (heapEntry.version !== groupVersions[heapEntry.group]) {
        continue;
      }
      const group = heapEntry.group;
      const rep = heapEntry.row;
      if (assignment[rep] >= 0) {
        continue;
      }

      const outIdx = selected.length;
      selected.push(rep);
      assignment[rep] = outIdx;
      groupReps[group].push(outIdx);
      state.removeSelectedCandidate(group);
      state.updateCachedSimilaritiesForGroup(group, rep);
      pushGroupBest(group);
    }
  }

  const activeCovarianceTerms = getCovarianceTerms();
  const assignmentSimilarityIfAbove =
    activeCovarianceTerms && rgb
      ? gaussianLogSimilarityRgbCovIfAbove
      : gaussianLogSimilarityIfAbove;

  const buildSelectedGroupReps = () => {
    const repsByGroup = new Array(groupCount);
    for (let slot = 0; slot < selected.length; slot++) {
      const row = selected[slot];
      const group = groupForFlat[pointFlats[row]];
      if (group < 0) {
        continue;
      }
      let reps = repsByGroup[group];
      if (!reps) {
        reps = [];
        repsByGroup[group] = reps;
      }
      reps.push(slot);
    }
    return repsByGroup;
  };

  const buildNeighborRepRows = (primaryRepsByGroup, extraRepsByGroup = null) => {
    const slotArrays = new Array(groupCount);
    const rowArrays = new Array(groupCount);
    const seen = new Int32Array(selected.length);
    seen.fill(-1);

    const appendNeighborSlots = (group, repsByGroup, slots) => {
      if (!repsByGroup) {
        return;
      }
      const neighborGroups = neighborGroupsByGroup[group];
      for (let i = 0; i < neighborGroups.length; i++) {
        const neighborReps = repsByGroup[neighborGroups[i]];
        if (!neighborReps) {
          continue;
        }
        for (let r = 0; r < neighborReps.length; r++) {
          const slot = neighborReps[r];
          if (seen[slot] === group) {
            continue;
          }
          seen[slot] = group;
          slots.push(slot);
        }
      }
    };

    for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
      const slots = [];
      appendNeighborSlots(group, primaryRepsByGroup, slots);
      appendNeighborSlots(group, extraRepsByGroup, slots);
      const slotArray = new Int32Array(slots.length);
      const rowArray = new Int32Array(slots.length);
      for (let i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
        const slot = slots[i];
        slotArray[i] = slot;
        rowArray[i] = selected[slot];
      }
      slotArrays[group] = slotArray;
      rowArrays[group] = rowArray;
    }
    return { slotArrays, rowArrays };
  };

  const assignRowsToNeighborReps = (neighborReps) => {
    for (let group = 0; group < groupCount; group++) {
      const start = groupOffsets[group];
      const end = groupOffsets[group + 1];
      if (end <= start) {
        continue;
      }
      const reps = neighborReps.slotArrays[group];
      const repRows = neighborReps.rowArrays[group];
      if (reps.length === 0) {
        continue;
      }
      if (reps.length === 1) {
        for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
          assignment[groupIndices[p]] = reps[0];
        }
        continue;
      }
      for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
        const orig = groupIndices[p];
        let bestSlot = reps[0];
        let bestLogSimilarity = -Infinity;
        for (let r = 0; r < reps.length; r++) {
          const slot = reps[r];
          const rep = repRows[r];
          const logSimilarity = assignmentSimilarityIfAbove(
            activeCovarianceTerms,
            pos,
            rgb,
            orig,
            rep,
            voxelDiagSq,
            bestLogSimilarity,
          );
          if (logSimilarity > bestLogSimilarity) {
            bestLogSimilarity = logSimilarity;
            bestSlot = slot;
            if (bestLogSimilarity >= 0.0) {
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        assignment[orig] = bestSlot;
      }
    }
  };

  const assignedClusterMedoid = (
    clusterRows,
    start,
    end,
    fallbackRow,
    slot,
    selectedRowOwner,
  ) => {
    if (end <= start) {
      return fallbackRow;
    }
    if (end - start === 1) {
      return clusterRows[start];
    }

    let totalWeight = 0.0;
    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
      const row = clusterRows[i];
      const weight = Number.isFinite(coarseWeights[row])
        ? Math.max(coarseWeights[row], 1e-12)
        : 1e-12;
      totalWeight += weight;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(totalWeight) || totalWeight <= 0.0) {
      return fallbackRow;
    }

    let bestRow = -1;
    let bestCost = Infinity;
    let bestMedoidCost = Infinity;
    let bestWeight = -Infinity;
    let bestRadius = -Infinity;
    const invTotalWeight = 1.0 / totalWeight;
    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
      const row = clusterRows[i];
      const owner = selectedRowOwner.get(row);
      if (owner !== undefined && owner !== slot) {
        continue;
      }
      const weight = Number.isFinite(coarseWeights[row])
        ? Math.max(coarseWeights[row], 1e-12)
        : 1e-12;
      const radius = Number.isFinite(origRadius[row])
        ? origRadius[row]
        : -Infinity;
      let cost = 0.0;
      for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
        const other = clusterRows[j];
        const otherWeight = Number.isFinite(coarseWeights[other])
          ? Math.max(coarseWeights[other], 1e-12)
          : 1e-12;
        const logSimilarity = gaussianLogSimilarity(
          activeCovarianceTerms,
          pos,
          rgb,
          row,
          other,
          voxelDiagSq,
        );
        cost += otherWeight * candidateDistanceFromLog(logSimilarity);
        if ((cost * invTotalWeight) / weight > bestCost + 1e-12) {
          break;
        }
      }
      const medoidCost = cost * invTotalWeight;
      const weightedCost = medoidCost / weight;
      const costTied =
        weightedCost === bestCost ||
        Math.abs(weightedCost - bestCost) <= 1e-12;
      const medoidTied =
        medoidCost === bestMedoidCost ||
        Math.abs(medoidCost - bestMedoidCost) <= 1e-12;
      if (
        bestRow < 0 ||
        weightedCost < bestCost - 1e-12 ||
        (costTied &&
          (medoidCost < bestMedoidCost - 1e-12 ||
            (medoidTied &&
              (weight > bestWeight + 1e-12 ||
                (Math.abs(weight - bestWeight) <= 1e-12 &&
                  (radius > bestRadius + 1e-12 ||
                    (Math.abs(radius - bestRadius) <= 1e-12 &&
                      row > bestRow)))))))
      ) {
        bestRow = row;
        bestCost = weightedCost;
        bestMedoidCost = medoidCost;
        bestWeight = weight;
        bestRadius = radius;
      }
    }
    return bestRow >= 0 ? bestRow : fallbackRow;
  };

  const refineSelectedRowsWithAssignedMedoids = () => {
    const selectedCount = selected.length;
    const counts = new Uint32Array(selectedCount);
    for (let row = 0; row < n; row++) {
      const slot = assignment[row];
      if (slot >= 0 && slot < selectedCount) {
        counts[slot] += 1;
      }
    }

    const offsets = new Uint32Array(selectedCount + 1);
    for (let slot = 0; slot < selectedCount; slot++) {
      offsets[slot + 1] = offsets[slot] + counts[slot];
    }
    const clusterRows = new Int32Array(offsets[selectedCount]);
    const cursors = new Uint32Array(offsets);
    for (let row = 0; row < n; row++) {
      const slot = assignment[row];
      if (slot >= 0 && slot < selectedCount) {
        clusterRows[cursors[slot]++] = row;
      }
    }

    const selectedRowOwner = new Map();
    for (let slot = 0; slot < selectedCount; slot++) {
      selectedRowOwner.set(selected[slot], slot);
    }
    for (let slot = 0; slot < selectedCount; slot++) {
      const currentRow = selected[slot];
      const nextRow = assignedClusterMedoid(
        clusterRows,
        offsets[slot],
        offsets[slot + 1],
        currentRow,
        slot,
        selectedRowOwner,
      );
      if (nextRow !== currentRow) {
        if (selectedRowOwner.get(currentRow) === slot) {
          selectedRowOwner.delete(currentRow);
        }
        selectedRowOwner.set(nextRow, slot);
        selected[slot] = nextRow;
      }
    }
  };

  let neighborReps = buildNeighborRepRows(groupReps);
  assignRowsToNeighborReps(neighborReps);
  if (refineAssignedMedoids) {
    refineSelectedRowsWithAssignedMedoids();
    neighborReps = buildNeighborRepRows(groupReps, buildSelectedGroupReps());
    assignRowsToNeighborReps(neighborReps);
  }

  return {
    selected,
    assignment,
    origRadius: returnOrigRadius ? origRadius : null,
    voxelDiag,
  };
}

module.exports = {
  normalizeSplatTargetCount,
  constrainTargetSplatCount,
  planSimplifyCloudVoxel,
};
```

### src/tiling/splat-covariance.js
```
function normalizeQuaternionInScratch(quat) {
  let x = quat[0];
  let y = quat[1];
  let z = quat[2];
  let w = quat[3];
  const len2 = x * x + y * y + z * z + w * w;
  if (len2 < 1e-20) {
    return [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0];
  }
  const inv = 1.0 / Math.sqrt(len2);
  return [x * inv, y * inv, z * inv, w * inv];
}

function covarianceComponentsFromScratch(scaleLogIn, quatIn, out) {
  const [x, y, z, w] = normalizeQuaternionInScratch(quatIn);
  const xx = x * x;
  const yy = y * y;
  const zz = z * z;
  const xy = x * y;
  const xz = x * z;
  const yz = y * z;
  const wx = w * x;
  const wy = w * y;
  const wz = w * z;

  const s0 = Math.exp(scaleLogIn[0]);
  const s1 = Math.exp(scaleLogIn[1]);
  const s2 = Math.exp(scaleLogIn[2]);
  const s2x = s0 * s0;
  const s2y = s1 * s1;
  const s2z = s2 * s2;

  const r00 = 1.0 - 2.0 * (yy + zz);
  const r10 = 2.0 * (xy - wz);
  const r20 = 2.0 * (xz + wy);
  const r01 = 2.0 * (xy + wz);
  const r11 = 1.0 - 2.0 * (xx + zz);
  const r21 = 2.0 * (yz - wx);
  const r02 = 2.0 * (xz - wy);
  const r12 = 2.0 * (yz + wx);
  const r22 = 1.0 - 2.0 * (xx + yy);

  out[0] = r00 * r00 * s2x + r10 * r10 * s2y + r20 * r20 * s2z;
  out[1] = r00 * r01 * s2x + r10 * r11 * s2y + r20 * r21 * s2z;
  out[2] = r00 * r02 * s2x + r10 * r12 * s2y + r20 * r22 * s2z;
  out[3] = r01 * r01 * s2x + r11 * r11 * s2y + r21 * r21 * s2z;
  out[4] = r01 * r02 * s2x + r11 * r12 * s2y + r21 * r22 * s2z;
  out[5] = r02 * r02 * s2x + r12 * r12 * s2y + r22 * r22 * s2z;
  return out;
}

function quaternionFromRotationMatrix(
  r00,
  r01,
  r02,
  r10,
  r11,
  r12,
  r20,
  r21,
  r22,
) {
  const trace = r00 + r11 + r22;
  let x;
  let y;
  let z;
  let w;

  if (trace > 0.0) {
    const s = Math.sqrt(trace + 1.0) * 2.0;
    w = 0.25 * s;
    x = (r21 - r12) / s;
    y = (r02 - r20) / s;
    z = (r10 - r01) / s;
  } else if (r00 > r11 && r00 > r22) {
    const s = Math.sqrt(Math.max(1.0 + r00 - r11 - r22, 1e-20)) * 2.0;
    w = (r21 - r12) / s;
